TC_GENERATED_CLOCK Actived create_generated_clock -name video165m -source [get_ports {clk}] -multiply_by 33 -divide_by 10 -phase 0 -duty_cycle 50 [get_pins {video_pll/u_pll/PLLA_inst/CLKOUT1}]
TC_GENERATED_CLOCK Actived create_generated_clock -name pixel33m -source [get_pins {video_pll/u_pll/PLLA_inst/CLKOUT1}] -master_clock video165m -divide_by 5 [get_pins {pixel_divider/CLKOUT}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_ports {stop_n}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -to [get_pins {*reset*/pipe*/CLEAR}]
TC_REPORT_TIMING Actived report_timing -setup -max_paths 50
TC_REPORT_TIMING Actived report_timing -hold -max_paths 50
TC_REPORT_TIMING Actived report_timing -recovery -max_paths 50
TC_REPORT_TIMING Actived report_timing -removal -max_paths 50
